```html
            background: var(--card-bg); border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding:1.8rem 2rem; margin-bottom:1.8rem;
            border:1px solid var(--border); box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
            transition:var(--transition);
        }
        .card:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); }
        .card h3 { font-size:1.1rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.6rem; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; }
        .card p, .card li { color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.92rem; line-height:1.7; }
        .card ul { padding-left:1.6rem; }
        .card ul li { margin-bottom:0.35rem; }

        /* Tags */
        .tag {
            display:inline-block; font-size:0.7rem; font-weight:600;
            padding:0.18rem 0.6rem; border-radius:5px; letter-spacing:0.02em;
            margin-right:0.3rem; vertical-align:middle;
        }
        .tag-diff { background:var(--diffusion-light); color:var(--diffusion); border:1px solid rgba(211,84,0,0.3); }
        .tag-seg { background:var(--segmentation-light); color:var(--segmentation); border:1px solid rgba(142,68,173,0.3); }
        .tag-parallel { background:var(--parallel-light); color:var(--parallel-accent); border:1px solid rgba(230,126,34,0.3); }
        .tag-serial { background:var(--serial-light); color:var(--serial-accent); border:1px solid rgba(91,76,196,0.3); }
        .tag-masson { background:var(--masson-light); color:var(--masson); }
        .tag-pas { background:var(--pas-light); color:var(--pas); }
        .tag-pasm { background:var(--pasm-light); color:var(--pasm); }
        .tag-qc { background:var(--qc-light); color:var(--qc); }

        /* SVG diagrams container */
        .diagram-wrap {
            margin:1.2rem 0; border-radius:var(--radius-md);
            background: #fdfdfc; border:1px solid #ece7dd;
            padding:0.5rem; overflow-x:auto;
        }
        .diagram-wrap svg { width:100%; height:auto; display:block; min-width:700px; }

        /* Compare grid */
        .compare-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:1.4rem; margin-bottom:1.8rem; }
        @media (max-width:700px) { .compare-grid { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .compare-card {
            background:var(--card-bg); border-radius:var(--radius-lg);
            padding:1.7rem 1.8rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm); transition:var(--transition);
        }
        .compare-card.parallel { border-top:4px solid var(--parallel-accent); }
        .compare-card.serial { border-top:4px solid var(--serial-accent); }
        .compare-card h3 { font-size:1.05rem; font-weight:700; }

        /* Highlights grid */
        .grid-3 { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr); gap:1.2rem; margin-bottom:1.4rem; }
        @media (max-width:800px) { .grid-3 { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .mini-card {
            background:var(--card-bg); border-radius:var(--radius-md);
            padding:1.3rem 1.4rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm); transition:var(--transition);
        }
        .mini-card:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .mini-card h4 { font-size:0.95rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.4rem; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.4rem; }

        .footer { text-align:center; padding:2rem 1rem; color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.82rem; border-top:1px solid var(--border); margin-top:2rem; }

        @keyframes fadeUp { from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(18px);} to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0);} }
        .animate { animation:fadeUp 0.5s ease-out forwards; }
        .delay-1 { animation-delay:0.05s; opacity:0; }
        .delay-2 { animation-delay:0.15s; opacity:0; }
        .delay-3 { animation-delay:0.25s; opacity:0; }
        .delay-4 { animation-delay:0.35s; opacity:0; }

        @media (max-width:500px) { .hero h1 { font-size:1.8rem; } }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
<nav class="top-nav">
    <span class="logo"><span class="logo-dot"></span>Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow</span>
    <span class="nav-links">
        <a href="#arch">Architecture</a>
        <a href="#stages">Stages</a>
        <a href="#parallel-vs-serial">Parallel vs Serial</a>
        <a href="#highlights">Highlights</a>
    </span>
</nav>

<div class="main-container">

    <!-- Hero -->
    <section class="hero animate">
        <span class="badge">🧪 Renal Pathology · Multi‑Agent + Generative + Segmentation</span>
        <h1>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent <span class="highlight">Enhanced Workflow</span></h1>
        <p class="subtitle">
            <strong>Masson input → LDM diffusion generates PAS & PASM → MSCWNet segments lesions → 3 diagnosis experts + iterative QC → final report.</strong>
            Designed to replicate clinical pathology logic: stain generation, precise lesion localization, cross‑stain verification, and hallucination suppression.
        </p>
        <div class="metrics-row">
            <span class="metric-chip"><span class="dot-mini" style="background:var(--masson);"></span> Masson Input</span>
            <span class="metric-chip"><span class="dot-mini" style="background:var(--diffusion);"></span> LDM Generation</span>
            <span class="metric-chip"><span class="dot-mini" style="background:var(--segmentation);"></span> MSCWNet</span>
            <span class="metric-chip"><span class="dot-mini" style="background:var(--qc);"></span> QC Expert</span>
            <span class="metric-chip">🔄 ≤2 Iterations</span>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Architecture Overview -->
    <section id="arch" class="animate delay-1">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="num num-neutral">◆</span> Enhanced Architecture Overview</div>
        <p class="section-subtitle">End‑to‑end pipeline: single Masson input → generation → segmentation → multi‑expert diagnosis → iterative QC</p>
        <div class="card" style="padding:0.8rem 1rem;">
            <div class="diagram-wrap">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 1300 720" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" style="font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;">
                    <defs>
                        <filter id="cardShadow" x="-8%" y="-8%" width="120%" height="130%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="3" stdDeviation="4" flood-color="#1a1714" flood-opacity="0.06"/></filter>
                        <filter id="cardShadowDeep" x="-8%" y="-8%" width="120%" height="130%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="5" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#1a1714" flood-opacity="0.10"/></filter>
                        <marker id="arrowDiff" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#d35400"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowSeg" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#8e44ad"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowRed" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#c0392b"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowBlue" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#1f618d"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowGreen" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#1e7e4b"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowPurple" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#7d3c98"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowOrange" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#e67e22"/></marker>
                        <marker id="arrowDark" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="6" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,3 L0,6 L1.5,3 Z" fill="#2c3e50"/></marker>
                        <linearGradient id="gradDiff" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#e67e22"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#d35400"/></linearGradient>
                        <linearGradient id="gradSeg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#9b59b6"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#8e44ad"/></linearGradient>
                        <linearGradient id="gradMasson" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#e74c3c"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#c0392b"/></linearGradient>
                        <linearGradient id="gradPAS" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#3498db"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#1f618d"/></linearGradient>
                        <linearGradient id="gradPASM" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#2ecc71"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#1e7e4b"/></linearGradient>
                        <linearGradient id="gradQC" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#a569bd"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#7d3c98"/></linearGradient>
                    </defs>
                    <rect width="1300" height="720" fill="#fdfdfc" rx="16"/>

                    <!-- Input -->
                    <text x="60" y="36" font-size="14" font-weight="700" fill="#1a1714">📥 Input: Masson (608×608)</text>
                    <rect x="60" y="52" width="120" height="82" rx="12" fill="#fef4f2" stroke="#c0392b" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="120" y="88" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#c0392b">Masson</text>
                    <text x="120" y="108" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#8b3a3a">Collagen / Fibrosis</text>
                    <text x="120" y="124" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#aaa">Original</text>

                    <!-- Arrow to LDM -->
                    <path d="M180,93 C220,93 240,80 270,80" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>

                    <!-- LDM -->
                    <rect x="280" y="38" width="210" height="110" rx="14" fill="#fef6f0" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="385" y="68" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#d35400">🧬 LDM Stain Gen</text>
                    <text x="385" y="88" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#a04000">Latent Diffusion Model</text>
                    <text x="385" y="106" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#a04000">Masson → PAS + PASM</text>
                    <text x="385" y="124" text-anchor="middle" font-size="8.5" fill="#aaa">Parallel generation</text>

                    <!-- Arrows to PAS/PASM -->
                    <path d="M490,80 C530,80 540,55 570,55" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>
                    <path d="M490,105 C530,105 540,140 570,140" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>

                    <!-- PAS -->
                    <rect x="580" y="32" width="110" height="60" rx="10" fill="#f2f7fb" stroke="#1f618d" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="635" y="58" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#1f618d">PAS</text>
                    <text x="635" y="78" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#3a5a7b">Generated</text>

                    <!-- PASM -->
                    <rect x="580" y="115" width="110" height="60" rx="10" fill="#f2faf6" stroke="#1e7e4b" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="635" y="141" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#1e7e4b">PASM</text>
                    <text x="635" y="161" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#2a5a3a">Generated</text>

                    <!-- Parallel label -->
                    <rect x="520" y="82" width="40" height="28" rx="8" fill="#fef8f3" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="1.8"/>
                    <text x="540" y="100" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#e67e22">PAR</text>

                    <!-- Arrow to segmentation -->
                    <path d="M690,62 C720,62 730,90 750,90" fill="none" stroke="#8e44ad" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowSeg)"/>
                    <path d="M690,145 C720,145 730,100 750,100" fill="none" stroke="#8e44ad" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowSeg)"/>

                    <!-- Also Masson to segmentation -->
                    <path d="M180,93 C240,93 250,170 300,180" fill="none" stroke="#8e44ad" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="6,4" marker-end="url(#arrowSeg)"/>

                    <!-- MSCWNet -->
                    <rect x="760" y="55" width="230" height="90" rx="14" fill="#faf5fd" stroke="#8e44ad" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="875" y="85" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#8e44ad">🔬 MSCWNet Seg.</text>
                    <text x="875" y="105" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#6c3483">CNN + Wavelet + Multi-scale Att.</text>
                    <text x="875" y="122" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#6c3483">Lesion masks (3 stains)</text>
                    <text x="875" y="138" text-anchor="middle" font-size="8.5" fill="#aaa">Parallel segmentation</text>

                    <!-- Arrows to diagnosis experts -->
                    <path d="M990,80 C1040,80 1060,40 1090,40" fill="none" stroke="#c0392b" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#arrowRed)"/>
                    <path d="M990,100 C1040,100 1060,120 1090,120" fill="none" stroke="#1f618d" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#arrowBlue)"/>
                    <path d="M990,120 C1040,120 1060,200 1090,200" fill="none" stroke="#1e7e4b" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#arrowGreen)"/>

                    <!-- Expert A -->
                    <rect x="1100" y="18" width="170" height="60" rx="10" fill="#fff" stroke="#c0392b" stroke-width="2.3" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="1185" y="44" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#c0392b">Expert A (Masson)</text>
                    <text x="1185" y="62" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#555">+ lesion mask</text>

                    <!-- Expert B -->
                    <rect x="1100" y="98" width="170" height="60" rx="10" fill="#fff" stroke="#1f618d" stroke-width="2.3" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="1185" y="124" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#1f618d">Expert B (PAS)</text>
                    <text x="1185" y="142" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#555">+ lesion mask</text>

                    <!-- Expert C -->
                    <rect x="1100" y="178" width="170" height="60" rx="10" fill="#fff" stroke="#1e7e4b" stroke-width="2.3" filter="url(#cardShadow)"/>
                    <text x="1185" y="204" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#1e7e4b">Expert C (PASM)</text>
                    <text x="1185" y="222" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#555">+ lesion mask</text>

                    <!-- Parallel indicator -->
                    <rect x="1280" y="100" width="20" height="110" rx="10" fill="#fef8f3" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="2"/>
                    <text x="1290" y="160" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#e67e22" transform="rotate(90,1290,160)">PARALLEL</text>

                    <!-- Merge to initial report -->
                    <path d="M1185,238 C1185,280 900,280 700,280 C600,280 500,280 450,300" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowDark)"/>

                    <rect x="310" y="300" width="280" height="55" rx="12" fill="#f0f3f6" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadowDeep)"/>
                    <text x="450" y="325" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#2c3e50">📋 Initial Report</text>
                    <text x="450" y="344" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#4a443c">Pure fact concatenation from 3 experts</text>

                    <!-- QC -->
                    <path d="M450,355 L450,400" fill="none" stroke="#7d3c98" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowPurple)"/>
                    <rect x="270" y="410" width="360" height="80" rx="14" fill="#f9f4fc" stroke="#7d3c98" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#cardShadowDeep)"/>
                    <text x="450" y="440" text-anchor="middle" font-size="14" font-weight="700" fill="#7d3c98">🔍 QC Expert (Qwen3‑VL‑30B)</text>
                    <text x="450" y="462" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#5c3a6b">3‑layer verification: Completeness · Consistency · Field‑of‑View</text>
                    <text x="450" y="480" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9.5" fill="#8a7e6e">Evidence priority: PASM > PAS > Masson</text>

                    <!-- Feedback loop -->
                    <path d="M640,450 C720,450 750,380 750,300 C750,220 700,180 600,160" fill="none" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-dasharray="8,5" marker-end="url(#arrowOrange)"/>
                    <rect x="680" y="340" width="120" height="36" rx="18" fill="#fef8f3" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="1.8"/>
                    <text x="740" y="363" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="#e67e22">🔄 Re‑inspect</text>

                    <!-- Final output -->
                    <path d="M450,490 L450,540" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.8" marker-end="url(#arrowDark)"/>
                    <rect x="270" y="555" width="360" height="50" rx="12" fill="#2c3e50" filter="url(#cardShadowDeep)"/>
                    <text x="450" y="588" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#ffffff">📄 Final Diagnostic Report (Traceable · Auditable)</text>

                    <!-- Legend -->
                    <rect x="30" y="620" width="500" height="70" rx="10" fill="#fdfdfc" stroke="#e3ded3" stroke-width="1"/>
                    <text x="50" y="645" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="#1a1714">Legend:</text>
                    <rect x="120" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#d35400"/>
                    <text x="138" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">LDM</text>
                    <rect x="180" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#8e44ad"/>
                    <text x="198" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">MSCWNet</text>
                    <rect x="270" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#c0392b"/>
                    <text x="288" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">Masson</text>
                    <rect x="340" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#1f618d"/>
                    <text x="358" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">PAS</text>
                    <rect x="390" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#1e7e4b"/>
                    <text x="408" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">PASM</text>
                    <rect x="450" y="635" width="12" height="8" rx="2" fill="#7d3c98"/>
                    <text x="468" y="644" font-size="9" fill="#555">QC</text>
                    <line x1="120" y1="665" x2="140" y2="665" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="6,3"/>
                    <text x="148" y="669" font-size="9" fill="#555">Feedback loop</text>
                </svg>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Detailed Stages -->
    <section id="stages" class="animate delay-2">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="num num-neutral">☰</span> Detailed Workflow Stages</div>
        <p class="section-subtitle">From a single Masson patch to a validated, evidence‑bound diagnostic report</p>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid #c0392b;">
            <h3>📥 Stage 0: Masson Input</h3>
            <p>A single <strong>608×608 Masson</strong> trichrome stained patch is supplied. The system will generate the two missing special stains before any analysis begins.</p>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--diffusion);">
            <h3>🧬 Stage 1: Missing Stain Generation <span class="tag tag-diff">LDM</span> <span class="tag tag-parallel">Parallel</span></h3>
            <p>A <strong>Latent Diffusion Model</strong> trained on aligned Masson‑PAS‑PASM triplets synthesizes PAS and PASM from the input Masson. This eliminates the need for additional physical staining while guaranteeing perfect spatial alignment.</p>
            <div class="diagram-wrap">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 600 150" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                    <rect x="20" y="40" width="90" height="70" rx="10" fill="#fef4f2" stroke="#c0392b" stroke-width="2.5"/>
                    <text x="65" y="80" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#c0392b">Masson</text>
                    <text x="65" y="98" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#888">Input</text>
                    <path d="M110,75 L160,75" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>
                    <rect x="170" y="20" width="170" height="110" rx="12" fill="#fef6f0" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="2.5"/>
                    <text x="255" y="55" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#d35400">LDM</text>
                    <text x="255" y="75" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#a04000">Latent Diffusion</text>
                    <text x="255" y="95" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#a04000">Cross‑stain translation</text>
                    <text x="255" y="115" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#a04000">Masson→PAS + Masson→PASM</text>
                    <path d="M340,50 L390,35" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>
                    <path d="M340,100 L390,115" fill="none" stroke="#d35400" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrowDiff)"/>
                    <rect x="400" y="25" width="90" height="45" rx="8" fill="#f2f7fb" stroke="#1f618d" stroke-width="2.2"/>
                    <text x="445" y="53" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#1f618d">PAS</text>
                    <rect x="400" y="90" width="90" height="45" rx="8" fill="#f2faf6" stroke="#1e7e4b" stroke-width="2.2"/>
                    <text x="445" y="118" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#1e7e4b">PASM</text>
                </svg>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--segmentation);">
            <h3>🔬 Stage 2: Lesion Segmentation <span class="tag tag-seg">MSCWNet</span> <span class="tag tag-parallel">Parallel</span></h3>
            <p><strong>MSCWNet</strong> (Multi‑Scale Convolution with Wavelet attention) processes the three aligned stain images simultaneously, producing precise lesion masks. Wavelet transforms capture fine textures while multi‑scale attention aggregates contextual cues.</p>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--parallel-accent);">
            <h3>🤖 Stage 3: Parallel Multi‑Expert Diagnosis <span class="tag tag-parallel">Parallel</span></h3>
            <p>Three independent diagnosis experts (Patho‑R1 7B) each receive one stain image + its lesion mask. They work <strong>concurrently</strong>, each constrained to its own stain's observable features. Results are merged into an initial report as pure facts, without any subjective inference.</p>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--serial-accent);">
            <h3>🔄 Stage 4: Iterative Cross‑Verification QC <span class="tag tag-serial">Serial</span> <span class="tag tag-qc">≤2 rounds</span></h3>
            <p>The Qwen3‑VL‑30B QC expert verifies the report with three checks: <strong>Completeness</strong> (mandatory fields), <strong>Cross‑stain Consistency</strong> (e.g., PASM “spikes” must match PAS “thickening”), and <strong>Field‑of‑View Limits</strong> (no “entire kidney” hallucinations). Failures trigger a targeted re‑inspection of only the relevant stain.</p>
        </div>

        <div class="card" style="border-left:5px solid var(--report);">
            <h3>📄 Stage 5: Evidence‑Bound Final Report</h3>
            <p>Conclusions are integrated with priority <strong>PASM > PAS > Masson</strong>. Every statement is anchored to its source stain. The report is rendered via Jinja2 and served through a FastAPI endpoint.</p>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Parallel vs Serial -->
    <section id="parallel-vs-serial" class="animate delay-3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="num num-neutral">⇵</span> Parallel & Serial Execution in the Workflow</div>
        <div class="compare-grid">
            <div class="compare-card parallel">
                <h3>⚡ Parallel Stages</h3>
                <p><strong>Stain Generation:</strong> PAS & PASM generated simultaneously.</p>
                <p><strong>Segmentation:</strong> All 3 stains segmented in parallel.</p>
                <p><strong>Diagnosis:</strong> Experts A/B/C work concurrently.</p>
                <p style="font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-muted);">✅ Maximizes throughput; independent tasks never interfere.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="compare-card serial">
                <h3>🔄 Serial QC Loop</h3>
                <p><strong>Report → QC → Re‑inspect → Revised Report → QC …</strong></p>
                <p>Each iteration depends on the previous one; targeted corrections ensure convergence within ≤2 rounds.</p>
                <p style="font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-muted);">✅ Ensures reliability; each correction builds on the last.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Highlights -->
    <section id="highlights" class="animate delay-4">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="num num-neutral">★</span> Key Enhancements Over the Original Workflow</div>
        <div class="grid-3">
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--diffusion);"><h4>🧬 Single‑Stain Input</h4><p>Only Masson needed; LDM synthesizes PAS & PASM, reducing physical staining.</p></div>
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--segmentation);"><h4>🔬 Precise Lesion Focus</h4><p>MSCWNet provides masks, directing LLM attention to clinically relevant regions.</p></div>
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--parallel-accent);"><h4>⚡ Maximal Parallelism</h4><p>Generation, segmentation, and diagnosis all exploit independent parallelism.</p></div>
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--serial-accent);"><h4>🔄 Smart Serial QC</h4><p>Targeted re‑inspection only for failed checks; ≤2 iterations.</p></div>
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--qc);"><h4>📎 Evidence Binding</h4><p>Every conclusion traced to its stain source; hallucination drastically reduced.</p></div>
            <div class="mini-card" style="border-top:4px solid var(--success);"><h4>🖥️ Lightweight Deploy</h4><p>4‑bit quantization, staged loading; runs on research‑grade GPUs.</p></div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <footer class="footer">
        <p>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow with LDM Generation & MSCWNet Segmentation</p>
        <p style="margin-top:0.3rem;">Models: LDM (stain gen) · MSCWNet (segmentation) · Patho‑R1 7B (diagnosis) · Qwen3‑VL‑30B (QC) · Inference: vLLM 0.6.0+</p>
    </footer>
</div>

<script>
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-links a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    function updateActiveNav() {
        let current = '';
        const pos = window.scrollY + 80;
        sections.forEach(s => { if(s.offsetTop <= pos && s.offsetTop + s.offsetHeight > pos) current = s.id; });
        navLinks.forEach(l => { l.classList.remove('active'); if(l.getAttribute('href')==='#'+current) l.classList.add('active'); });
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', updateActiveNav, {passive:true});
    updateActiveNav();

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(e => { if(e.isIntersecting) { e.target.style.opacity='1'; e.target.style.transform='translateY(0)'; } });
    }, {threshold:0.08});
    document.querySelectorAll('.animate').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
### 工作流展示解析

这个页面围绕您的需求，对流程图的视觉呈现和整体设计做了强化，希望能清晰传达您工作流的核心逻辑。

- **全局架构图 (SVG)**：使用**分层、分色块**的布局展示从 Masson 输入到最终报告的完整链路。**平滑的曲线箭头**和**渐变填充**清晰区分了 LDM、MSCWNet、诊断专家和质控专家等不同阶段，并用独立的“PARALLEL”标签和虚线反馈回路突出并行与串行逻辑。
- **流程阶段卡片**：每个核心步骤（输入、染色生成、病灶分割、诊断、质控、报告）都设计为**独立的卡片**，左侧配有该阶段专属的彩色边框。卡片内嵌了**简化的流程图**，便于对照文字理解。
- **并行与串行对比**：通过**双栏布局**直观对比工作流中**并行阶段**（染色生成、分割、诊断）与**串行阶段**（质控迭代）的不同特点与优势，突出设计思想。
- **视觉与细节**：整体采用**暖色渐变背景**、柔和的阴影和圆角，图表使用**统一的彩色标签和指示线**，并添加了图例说明，提升了页面的**精致感和可读性**。

---

**优化建议：** 页面中的 LDM 模型、MSCWNet 分割模型名称及诊断质控模型均基于您描述占位。您可根据实际使用的具体模型路径或名称，在卡片文字和 SVG 图表中直接修改。

### DeepSeek

这是为您重新设计的全英文品牌形象页面，采用现代卡片与时间线布局，突出流程逻辑与增强优势，摒弃了传统方框堆砌的样式。
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f9f8f6;
            --surface: #ffffff;
            --text: #1e1c19;
            --text-secondary: #5b554b;
            --text-muted: #8c857b;
            --border: #e7e2d8;
            --shadow-sm: 0 2px 8px rgba(30,28,25,0.04);
            --shadow-md: 0 8px 24px rgba(30,28,25,0.06);
            --shadow-lg: 0 16px 40px rgba(30,28,25,0.09);
            --masson: #c44536;
            --pas: #206a8a;
            --pasm: #217d4b;
            --qc: #7d3c98;
            --report: #2c3e50;
            --diffusion: #d35400;
            --segmentation: #8e44ad;
            --parallel: #e67e22;
            --serial: #5b4cc4;
            --radius-xs: 6px;
            --radius-sm: 10px;
            --radius-md: 16px;
            --radius-lg: 24px;
            --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', 'PingFang SC', 'Microsoft YaHei', sans-serif;
            --transition: 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
        }

        * { margin:0; padding:0; box-sizing:border-box; }
        html { scroll-behavior:smooth; font-size:15px; }
        body {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            line-height:1.6;
            -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 20% 20%, rgba(211,84,0,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 80% 70%, rgba(142,68,173,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
        }

        /* Navigation */
        .top-nav {
            position: sticky; top:0; z-index:100;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
            padding:0 2.5rem; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:2rem;
            height:56px; box-shadow:0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03);
        }
        .logo {
            font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:-0.02em;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.6rem;
        }
        .logo-dot {
            width:9px; height:9px; border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--diffusion); animation: pulse 2s infinite;
        }
        @keyframes pulse { 0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(211,84,0,0.5);} 50%{box-shadow:0 0 0 12px rgba(211,84,0,0);} }
        .nav-links { display:flex; gap:0.4rem; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .nav-links a {
            text-decoration:none; color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.84rem;
            padding:0.35rem 0.8rem; border-radius:20px; transition:var(--transition);
            font-weight:500; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .nav-links a:hover { background:rgba(0,0,0,0.04); color:var(--text); }
        .nav-links a.active { background:rgba(0,0,0,0.07); font-weight:600; }

        .container { max-width:1200px; margin:0 auto; padding:2rem 2.5rem 4rem; }
        @media (max-width:768px) { .container { padding:1.5rem 1.2rem 3rem; } }

        /* Hero */
        .hero {
            text-align:center; padding:3rem 1rem 2.5rem;
            position:relative; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .hero::before {
            content:""; position:absolute; top:-100px; right:-100px;
            width:400px; height:400px; background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(211,84,0,0.06) 0%, transparent 70%);
            border-radius:50%; z-index:0;
        }
        .hero .badge {
            display:inline-block; background: #fff; color:var(--diffusion);
            font-size:0.78rem; font-weight:600; padding:0.3rem 1.1rem;
            border-radius:20px; margin-bottom:1rem; border:1px solid rgba(211,84,0,0.25);
            position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .hero h1 {
            font-size:2.8rem; font-weight:800; letter-spacing:-0.03em; line-height:1.2;
            margin-bottom:0.6rem; position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .hero h1 .highlight {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #d35400, #8e44ad);
            -webkit-background-clip:text; -webkit-text-fill-color:transparent; background-clip:text;
        }
        .hero .subtitle {
            font-size:1.05rem; color:var(--text-secondary); max-width:800px;
            margin:0 auto 1.8rem; position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .metrics {
            display:flex; gap:0.9rem; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:center;
            position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .metric {
            background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:30px;
            padding:0.5rem 1.2rem; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:500; color:var(--text-secondary);
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
            transition:var(--transition);
        }
        .metric:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); transform:translateY(-1px); }
        .dot { width:8px; height:8px; border-radius:50%; flex-shrink:0; }

        /* Section headers */
        .section-title {
            font-size:1.5rem; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:-0.02em;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.7rem; margin-bottom:0.4rem;
        }
        .section-title .idx {
            display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
            width:32px; height:32px; border-radius:10px; font-size:0.9rem;
            font-weight:700; color:#fff; background: #2c3e50;
        }
        .section-subtitle { color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.9rem; margin-bottom:1.6rem; margin-left:2.8rem; }

        /* Architecture SVG card */
        .arch-card {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:2rem;
            border:1px solid var(--border); box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
        }
        .arch-card svg { width:100%; height:auto; display:block; }

        /* Enhancements grid */
        .grid-3 {
            display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr); gap:1.5rem;
            margin-bottom:2rem;
        }
        @media (max-width:800px) { .grid-3 { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .enhance-card {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-md);
            padding:1.8rem 1.8rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm); transition:var(--transition);
            position:relative; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .enhance-card:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); transform:translateY(-3px); }
        .enhance-card .icon {
            font-size:2rem; margin-bottom:1rem; display:block;
            width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; background:#f5f3ef;
            display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
        }
        .enhance-card h4 { font-size:1.05rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.5rem; }
        .enhance-card p { font-size:0.88rem; color:var(--text-secondary); line-height:1.6; }

        /* Workflow stages timeline */
        .timeline {
            position:relative; padding-left:2rem; margin:2rem 0;
            border-left:2px solid var(--border);
        }
        .timeline-item {
            position:relative; margin-bottom:2.5rem; padding-left:2rem;
        }
        .timeline-item::before {
            content:""; position:absolute; left:-0.55rem; top:0.5rem;
            width:0.9rem; height:0.9rem; border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--surface); border:2px solid var(--text-muted);
        }
        .timeline-item.stage-diff::before { border-color: var(--diffusion); }
        .timeline-item.stage-seg::before { border-color: var(--segmentation); }
        .timeline-item.stage-par::before { border-color: var(--parallel); }
        .timeline-item.stage-serial::before { border-color: var(--serial); }
        .timeline-item.stage-report::before { border-color: var(--report); }
        .timeline-item h3 {
            font-size:1.2rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.2rem;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem;
        }
        .timeline-item .desc {
            color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.92rem; line-height:1.7;
            max-width:700px;
        }
        .tag {
            display:inline-block; font-size:0.68rem; font-weight:600;
            padding:0.15rem 0.6rem; border-radius:5px; letter-spacing:0.02em;
            background: #f0ede7; color: #4a443c;
        }

        /* Parallel vs Serial small compare */
        .compare-row {
            display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:1.5rem; margin:2rem 0;
        }
        @media (max-width:700px) { .compare-row { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .compare-block {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-md);
            padding:1.6rem 1.8rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
        }
        .compare-block.par { border-top:4px solid var(--parallel); }
        .compare-block.ser { border-top:4px solid var(--serial); }
        .compare-block h4 { font-size:1.1rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.8rem; }

        .footer { text-align:center; padding:2rem 1rem; color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.82rem; border-top:1px solid var(--border); margin-top:2rem; }

        @keyframes fadeUp { from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px);} to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0);} }
        .animate { animation:fadeUp 0.5s ease-out forwards; opacity:0; }
        .d1 { animation-delay:0.05s; } .d2 { animation-delay:0.15s; } .d3 { animation-delay:0.25s; } .d4 { animation-delay:0.35s; }
    </style>
</head>

    <!-- Key Enhancements -->
    <section id="enhance" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">★</span> Key Enhancements Over the Original</div>
        <div class="grid-3">
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#fef6f0;">🧬</span>
                <h4>Single‑Stain Input</h4>
                <p>Only Masson is required; LDM synthesizes PAS & PASM, reducing physical staining and guaranteeing spatial alignment.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#faf5fd;">🔬</span>
                <h4>Precise Lesion Focus</h4>
                <p>MSCWNet delivers lesion masks that guide the LLM’s attention to clinically relevant regions, improving diagnostic accuracy.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#fef8f3;">⚡</span>
                <h4>Maximal Parallelism</h4>
                <p>Stain generation, segmentation, and multi‑expert diagnosis all exploit independent parallelism for high throughput.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f5f4fd;">🔄</span>
                <h4>Smart Serial QC</h4>
                <p>Targeted re‑inspection only for failed consistency checks, converging within 2 iterations without full re‑runs.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f9f4fc;">📎</span>
                <h4>Evidence Binding</h4>
                <p>Every conclusion is anchored to its source stain, drastically reducing hallucinations and enabling clinical auditing.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f0f3f6;">🧠</span>
                <h4>Model Isolation</h4>
                <p>Diagnosis experts are constrained to their own stain, preventing cross‑contamination of features between modalities.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Workflow Stages Timeline -->
    <section id="stages" class="animate d4">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">☰</span> Workflow Stages</div>
        <div class="timeline">
            <div class="timeline-item stage-diff">
                <h3>🧬 Stage 1: Missing Stain Generation <span class="tag">LDM</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">A Latent Diffusion Model synthesizes PAS and PASM from the input Masson image, producing perfectly aligned triplets without additional physical staining.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-seg">
                <h3>🔬 Stage 2: Lesion Segmentation <span class="tag">MSCWNet</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">The CNN‑based MSCWNet processes all three stains simultaneously, leveraging wavelet transforms and multi‑scale attention to generate precise lesion masks.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-par">
                <h3>🤖 Stage 3: Parallel Multi‑Expert Diagnosis <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">Three independent Patho‑R1 experts, each receiving one stain and its lesion mask, work concurrently. Outputs are merged into a fact‑only initial report.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-serial">
                <h3>🔄 Stage 4: Iterative QC & Refinement <span class="tag">Serial</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">The Qwen3‑VL‑30B QC expert validates completeness, cross‑stain consistency, and field‑of‑view limits. Failures trigger a targeted re‑inspection loop (≤2 rounds).</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-report">
                <h3>📄 Stage 5: Evidence‑Bound Final Report</h3>
                <p class="desc">Conclusions are integrated with priority PASM > PAS > Masson and anchored to source stains. The report is rendered via Jinja2 and served through FastAPI.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Parallel vs Serial compare -->
    <section id="compare" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">⇵</span> Parallel & Serial Execution</div>
        <div class="compare-row">
            <div class="compare-block par">
                <h4>⚡ Parallel Stages</h4>
                <p><strong>Stain generation, segmentation, and diagnosis</strong> run simultaneously where dependencies allow, maximizing throughput.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="compare-block ser">
                <h4>🔄 Serial QC Loop</h4>
                <p>The <strong>QC → re‑inspect → revise</strong> cycle is strictly sequential, ensuring each correction builds on verified outputs.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <footer class="footer">
        <p>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow with LDM Generation & MSCWNet Segmentation</p>
        <p style="margin-top:0.3rem;">Models: LDM (stain gen) · MSCWNet (segmentation) · Patho‑R1 7B (diagnosis) · Qwen3‑VL‑30B (QC) · Inference: vLLM</p>
    </footer>
</div>

<script>
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-links a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        const pos = window.scrollY + 80;
        sections.forEach(s => { if(s.offsetTop <= pos && s.offsetTop + s.offsetHeight > pos) current = s.id; });
        navLinks.forEach(l => { l.classList.remove('active'); if(l.getAttribute('href')==='#'+current) l.classList.add('active'); });
    }, {passive:true});
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(e => { if(e.isIntersecting) { e.target.style.opacity='1'; e.target.style.transform='translateY(0)'; } });
    }, {threshold:0.08});
    document.querySelectorAll('.animate').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
### 工作流展示解析

这个页面围绕“流程逻辑”与“设计优势”重新组织了视觉叙事，希望能清晰传达您增强工作流的核心价值。

- **品牌化叙事入口**：顶部 **Hero 区域** 使用大标题与渐变高亮，配合微妙的径向光晕背景，直接点明“增强工作流”的主题。下方的**胶囊式指标**直观展示了从输入到质控的核心环节，替代了传统的项目符号。
- **清晰的架构全景图**：**SVG 流程图** 采用从左至右的线性布局，用不同颜色严格区分 LDM（橙）、MSCWNet（紫）、诊断专家（红/蓝/绿）和质控专家（紫）。**平滑的曲线箭头**避免了线条交叉混乱，并用“PARALLEL”括号和虚线反馈回路清晰标明了并行处理与串行迭代的逻辑。
- **优势卡片与时间线**：**六张增强卡片** 使用图标和悬浮效果，分点阐述“单染色输入”、“病灶聚焦”、“证据绑定”等设计优点，完全聚焦于流程价值。**工作流阶段**则用垂直时间线展示，每个阶段都有专属的彩色节点和标签，使“并行”与“串行”概念一目了然。
- **高级感排版细节**：整体采用**柔和阴影、大圆角卡片、细线边框**和**动态微交互**，避免了生硬的方框堆砌。对比区块用清晰的顶部色带区分，在保持信息密度的同时提升了精致感和可读性。

### 我（提问）

Enhanced Architecture Overview
这个图片这里的布局和设计都不行

Stage 2: Lesion Segmentation
MSCWNet
Parallel
MSCWNet (Multi‑Scale Convolution with Wavelet attention) processes the three aligned stain images simultaneously, producing precise lesion masks. Wavelet transforms capture fine textures while multi‑scale attention aggregates contextual cues.

🤖 Stage 3: Parallel Multi‑Expert Diagnosis
Parallel
Three independent diagnosis experts (Patho‑R1 7B) each receive one stain image + its lesion mask. They work concurrently, each constrained to its own stain's observable features. Results are merged into an initial report as pure facts, without any subjective inference.

🔄 Stage 4: Iterative Cross‑Verification QC
Serial
≤2 rounds
The Qwen3‑VL‑30B QC expert verifies the report with three checks: Completeness (mandatory fields), Cross‑stain Consistency (e.g., PASM “spikes” must match PAS “thickening”), and Field‑of‑View Limits (no “entire kidney” hallucinations). Failures trigger a targeted re‑inspection of only the relevant stain.

📄 Stage 5: Evidence‑Bound Final Report
Conclusions are integrated with priority PASM > PAS > Masson. Every statement is anchored to its source stain. The report is rendered via Jinja2 and served through a FastAPI endpoint.

⇵
Parallel & Serial Execution in the Workflow
⚡ Parallel Stages
Stain Generation: PAS & PASM generated simultaneously.

Segmentation: All 3 stains segmented in parallel.

Diagnosis: Experts A/B/C work concurrently.

✅ Maximizes throughput; independent tasks never interfere.

🔄 Serial QC Loop
Report → QC → Re‑inspect → Revised Report → QC …

Each iteration depends on the previous one; targeted corrections ensure convergence within ≤2 rounds.

✅ Ensures reliability; each correction builds on the last.

★
Key Enhancements Over the Original Workflow
🧬 Single‑Stain Input
Only Masson needed; LDM synthesizes PAS & PASM, reducing physical staining.

🔬 Precise Lesion Focus
MSCWNet provides masks, directing LLM attention to clinically relevant regions.

⚡ Maximal Parallelism
Generation, segmentation, and diagnosis all exploit independent parallelism.

🔄 Smart Serial QC
Targeted re‑inspection only for failed checks; ≤2 iterations.

📎 Evidence Binding
Every conclusion traced to its stain source; hallucination drastically reduced.

🖥️ Lightweight Deploy
4‑bit quantization, staged loading; runs on research‑grade GPUs.

Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow with LDM Generation & MSCWNet Segmentation

Models: LDM (stain gen) · MSCWNet (segmentation) · Patho‑R1 7B (diagnosis) · Qwen3‑VL‑30B (QC) · Inference: vLLM 0.6.0+

你这里的布局的部分也不行，很差很差，你可以好好思考一下如何重新设计布局，要充满设计感和高级感，而且你的流程图不能出现箭头乱的错误，你后面全都是方框和字，这样的就不行，你这样换颜色没有任何用处，丑死了，而且你主要是介绍这里的流程和方法，主要是介绍有点，你后面那个显卡什么的，那个没必要写，说优点，不写他的具体的方法，比如GPU的部署

### THINK

我们开始理解用户的反馈。用户对前一次回复中的架构图（Enhanced Architecture Overview）不满意，要求调整箭头。可能存在的问题包括箭头重叠、方向不清、线条交叉过多、箭头终点不明确等。用户希望箭头布局更清晰、更具设计感，避免混乱。我需要重新生成HTML，重点优化SVG架构图中的箭头路径和整体布局。

回顾上一次回复，SVG视图是1300x440，布局从左到右。但箭头可能仍有交叉或不够美观。用户说“箭头需要调整”，可能需要更清晰的流向，比如使用正交路径、避免斜线交叉、增加箭头标注等。我需要重新设计SVG，使数据流清晰，并行与串行区分明确。

根据用户之前的完整工作流：Masson输入 -> LDM生成PAS/PASM（并行） -> MSCWNet分割（接收三张图） -> 三个诊断专家（并行） -> 初始报告 -> QC（串行迭代） -> 最终报告。我需要用更优雅的箭头连接，可能使用直线加圆角拐弯，避免斜线交叉。另外，反馈回路（QC -> 重新检查）应该清晰且不与其他箭头重叠。

我将设计一个新的SVG架构图，采用更结构化的布局：水平分为多个阶段，垂直分层处理并行分支。比如：

- 左：Masson输入
- 中间上：LDM生成，分两路输出PAS和PASM
- 中间下：MSCWNet分割（同时接收Masson、PAS、PASM）
- 右侧：三个诊断专家垂直排列，用大括号表示并行
- 再右侧：初始报告，QC，最终报告，反馈箭头从QC绕回诊断专家附近，但不会太乱。

为了美观，箭头使用有圆角的路径`<path d="M... Q..."/>`，颜色保持对应模型。还可以加入“PARALLEL”和“SERIAL”标签。

此外，用户希望只突出流程和优点，不写GPU部署等，上一次已经移除了。这次只需要优化架构图，但同时要保持整体页面的设计感。由于用户只提到“这里的箭头需要调整”，我可以专注于修改那个SVG，其他部分保持（或微调）。但为了提供一个完整的解决方案，我会重新生成整个HTML文件，确保SVG中的箭头整洁。

我会在SVG中使用更清晰的网格布局，例如：

- 第1行：Masson -> LDM -> (分叉) PAS, PASM
- 第2行：Masson, PAS, PASM -> MSCWNet
- 第3行：MSCWNet -> 三个诊断专家 (并行)
- 第4行：三个专家 -> 初始报告 -> QC
- 第5行：QC -> 最终报告，以及反馈回路

箭头使用水平或垂直正交线，用`<path>`的`M ... L ...`形式，必要时使用圆角。用`marker-end`定义箭头。

同时要保持之前的设计感：卡片、阴影、渐变等。

最终我会呈现完整的HTML代码，替换掉之前的架构图部分。同时检查其他部分是否有箭头问题，比如Stage 2、Stage 3的简化图，但用户只提到了“Enhanced Architecture Overview”，所以只需修改那个大图。

这是为您重新设计的页面，重点优化了架构总览图的箭头路径与整体布局，使其更清晰、专业。
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f9f8f6;
            --surface: #ffffff;
            --text: #1e1c19;
            --text-secondary: #5b554b;
            --text-muted: #8c857b;
            --border: #e7e2d8;
            --shadow-sm: 0 2px 8px rgba(30,28,25,0.04);
            --shadow-md: 0 8px 24px rgba(30,28,25,0.06);
            --shadow-lg: 0 16px 40px rgba(30,28,25,0.09);
            --masson: #c44536;
            --pas: #206a8a;
            --pasm: #217d4b;
            --qc: #7d3c98;
            --report: #2c3e50;
            --diffusion: #d35400;
            --segmentation: #8e44ad;
            --parallel: #e67e22;
            --serial: #5b4cc4;
            --radius-xs: 6px;
            --radius-sm: 10px;
            --radius-md: 16px;
            --radius-lg: 24px;
            --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', 'PingFang SC', 'Microsoft YaHei', sans-serif;
            --transition: 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
        }

        * { margin:0; padding:0; box-sizing:border-box; }
        html { scroll-behavior:smooth; font-size:15px; }
        body {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            line-height:1.6;
            -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 20% 20%, rgba(211,84,0,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 80% 70%, rgba(142,68,173,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
        }

        /* Navigation */
        .top-nav {
            position: sticky; top:0; z-index:100;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
            padding:0 2.5rem; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:2rem;
            height:56px; box-shadow:0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03);
        }
        .logo {
            font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:-0.02em;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.6rem;
        }
        .logo-dot {
            width:9px; height:9px; border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--diffusion); animation: pulse 2s infinite;
        }
        @keyframes pulse { 0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(211,84,0,0.5);} 50%{box-shadow:0 0 0 12px rgba(211,84,0,0);} }
        .nav-links { display:flex; gap:0.4rem; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .nav-links a {
            text-decoration:none; color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.84rem;
            padding:0.35rem 0.8rem; border-radius:20px; transition:var(--transition);
            font-weight:500; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .nav-links a:hover { background:rgba(0,0,0,0.04); color:var(--text); }
        .nav-links a.active { background:rgba(0,0,0,0.07); font-weight:600; }

        .container { max-width:1200px; margin:0 auto; padding:2rem 2.5rem 4rem; }
        @media (max-width:768px) { .container { padding:1.5rem 1.2rem 3rem; } }

        /* Hero */
        .hero {
            text-align:center; padding:3rem 1rem 2.5rem;
            position:relative; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .hero::before {
            content:""; position:absolute; top:-100px; right:-100px;
            width:400px; height:400px; background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(211,84,0,0.06) 0%, transparent 70%);
            border-radius:50%; z-index:0;
        }
        .hero .badge {
            display:inline-block; background: #fff; color:var(--diffusion);
            font-size:0.78rem; font-weight:600; padding:0.3rem 1.1rem;
            border-radius:20px; margin-bottom:1rem; border:1px solid rgba(211,84,0,0.25);
            position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .hero h1 {
            font-size:2.8rem; font-weight:800; letter-spacing:-0.03em; line-height:1.2;
            margin-bottom:0.6rem; position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .hero h1 .highlight {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #d35400, #8e44ad);
            -webkit-background-clip:text; -webkit-text-fill-color:transparent; background-clip:text;
        }
        .hero .subtitle {
            font-size:1.05rem; color:var(--text-secondary); max-width:800px;
            margin:0 auto 1.8rem; position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .metrics {
            display:flex; gap:0.9rem; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:center;
            position:relative; z-index:1;
        }
        .metric {
            background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:30px;
            padding:0.5rem 1.2rem; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:500; color:var(--text-secondary);
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
            transition:var(--transition);
        }
        .metric:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); transform:translateY(-1px); }
        .dot { width:8px; height:8px; border-radius:50%; flex-shrink:0; }

        /* Section headers */
        .section-title {
            font-size:1.5rem; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:-0.02em;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.7rem; margin-bottom:0.4rem;
        }
        .section-title .idx {
            display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
            width:32px; height:32px; border-radius:10px; font-size:0.9rem;
            font-weight:700; color:#fff; background: #2c3e50;
        }
        .section-subtitle { color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.9rem; margin-bottom:1.6rem; margin-left:2.8rem; }

        /* Architecture card */
        .arch-card {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding:1rem; margin-bottom:2rem;
            border:1px solid var(--border); box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
            overflow-x: auto;
        }
        .arch-card svg { width:100%; height:auto; display:block; min-width:1000px; }

        /* Enhancements grid */
        .grid-3 {
            display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr); gap:1.5rem;
            margin-bottom:2rem;
        }
        @media (max-width:800px) { .grid-3 { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .enhance-card {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-md);
            padding:1.8rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm); transition:var(--transition);
            position:relative; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .enhance-card:hover { box-shadow:var(--shadow-md); transform:translateY(-3px); }
        .enhance-card .icon {
            font-size:2rem; margin-bottom:1rem; display:flex;
            align-items:center; justify-content:center;
            width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; background:#f5f3ef;
        }
        .enhance-card h4 { font-size:1.05rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.5rem; }
        .enhance-card p { font-size:0.88rem; color:var(--text-secondary); line-height:1.6; }

        /* Timeline */
        .timeline {
            position:relative; padding-left:2rem; margin:2rem 0;
            border-left:2px solid var(--border);
        }
        .timeline-item {
            position:relative; margin-bottom:2.5rem; padding-left:2rem;
        }
        .timeline-item::before {
            content:""; position:absolute; left:-0.55rem; top:0.5rem;
            width:0.9rem; height:0.9rem; border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--surface); border:2px solid var(--text-muted);
        }
        .timeline-item.stage-diff::before { border-color: var(--diffusion); }
        .timeline-item.stage-seg::before { border-color: var(--segmentation); }
        .timeline-item.stage-par::before { border-color: var(--parallel); }
        .timeline-item.stage-serial::before { border-color: var(--serial); }
        .timeline-item.stage-report::before { border-color: var(--report); }
        .timeline-item h3 {
            font-size:1.2rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.2rem;
            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem;
        }
        .timeline-item .desc {
            color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.92rem; line-height:1.7;
            max-width:700px;
        }
        .tag {
            display:inline-block; font-size:0.68rem; font-weight:600;
            padding:0.15rem 0.6rem; border-radius:5px; letter-spacing:0.02em;
            background: #f0ede7; color: #4a443c;
        }

        /* Compare */
        .compare-row {
            display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:1.5rem; margin:2rem 0;
        }
        @media (max-width:700px) { .compare-row { grid-template-columns:1fr; } }
        .compare-block {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-md);
            padding:1.6rem 1.8rem; border:1px solid var(--border);
            box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);
        }
        .compare-block.par { border-top:4px solid var(--parallel); }
        .compare-block.ser { border-top:4px solid var(--serial); }
        .compare-block h4 { font-size:1.1rem; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:0.8rem; }

        .footer { text-align:center; padding:2rem 1rem; color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.82rem; border-top:1px solid var(--border); margin-top:2rem; }

        @keyframes fadeUp { from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px);} to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0);} }
        .animate { animation:fadeUp 0.5s ease-out forwards; opacity:0; }
        .d1 { animation-delay:0.05s; } .d2 { animation-delay:0.15s; } .d3 { animation-delay:0.25s; } .d4 { animation-delay:0.35s; }
    </style>
</head>

<nav class="top-nav">

                <text x="570" y="295" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="#e67e22" transform="rotate(-90,570,295)">PARALLEL</text>

                <!-- Merge to Initial Report -->
                <path d="M775,225 L820,225 L820,295 L850,295" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#aDark)"/>
                <path d="M775,295 L850,295" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5"/>
                <path d="M775,365 L820,365 L820,295" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5"/>

                <rect x="860" y="270" width="140" height="50" rx="12" fill="#f0f3f6" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#shd)"/>
                <text x="930" y="300" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#2c3e50">Initial Report</text>

                <!-- QC -->
                <path d="M1000,295 L1030,295" fill="none" stroke="#7d3c98" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#aPurple)"/>
                <rect x="1040" y="270" width="120" height="50" rx="12" fill="#f9f4fc" stroke="#7d3c98" stroke-width="2.5" filter="url(#shd)"/>
                <text x="1100" y="300" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#7d3c98">QC Expert</text>

                <!-- Feedback loop (QC fails -> back to experts) -->
                <path d="M1100,320 L1100,400 L687,400 L687,390" fill="none" stroke="#e67e22" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="8,5" marker-end="url(#aOrange)"/>
                <text x="890" y="418" font-size="9" fill="#e67e22" font-weight="600">Re‑inspect (≤2 rounds)</text>

                <!-- Final report after QC pass -->
                <path d="M1160,295 L1180,295 L1180,90 L1050,90" fill="none" stroke="#2c3e50" stroke-width="2.5" marker-end="url(#aDark)"/>
                <rect x="930" y="75" width="110" height="30" rx="8" fill="#2c3e50"/>
                <text x="985" y="95" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="#fff">Final Report</text>
            </svg>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Key Enhancements -->
    <section id="enhance" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">★</span> Key Enhancements Over the Original</div>
        <div class="grid-3">
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#fef6f0;">🧬</span>
                <h4>Single‑Stain Input</h4>
                <p>Only Masson is required; LDM synthesizes PAS & PASM, reducing physical staining and guaranteeing spatial alignment.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#faf5fd;">🔬</span>
                <h4>Precise Lesion Focus</h4>
                <p>MSCWNet delivers lesion masks that guide the LLM’s attention to clinically relevant regions, improving diagnostic accuracy.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#fef8f3;">⚡</span>
                <h4>Maximal Parallelism</h4>
                <p>Stain generation, segmentation, and multi‑expert diagnosis all exploit independent parallelism for high throughput.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f5f4fd;">🔄</span>
                <h4>Smart Serial QC</h4>
                <p>Targeted re‑inspection only for failed consistency checks, converging within 2 iterations without full re‑runs.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f9f4fc;">📎</span>
                <h4>Evidence Binding</h4>
                <p>Every conclusion is anchored to its source stain, drastically reducing hallucinations and enabling clinical auditing.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="enhance-card">
                <span class="icon" style="background:#f0f3f6;">🧠</span>
                <h4>Model Isolation</h4>
                <p>Diagnosis experts are constrained to their own stain, preventing cross‑contamination of features between modalities.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Workflow Stages Timeline -->
    <section id="stages" class="animate d4">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">☰</span> Workflow Stages</div>
        <div class="timeline">
            <div class="timeline-item stage-diff">
                <h3>🧬 Stage 1: Missing Stain Generation <span class="tag">LDM</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">A Latent Diffusion Model synthesizes PAS and PASM from the input Masson image, producing perfectly aligned triplets without additional physical staining.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-seg">
                <h3>🔬 Stage 2: Lesion Segmentation <span class="tag">MSCWNet</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">The CNN‑based MSCWNet processes all three stains simultaneously, leveraging wavelet transforms and multi‑scale attention to generate precise lesion masks.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-par">
                <h3>🤖 Stage 3: Parallel Multi‑Expert Diagnosis <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">Three independent Patho‑R1 experts, each receiving one stain and its lesion mask, work concurrently. Outputs are merged into a fact‑only initial report.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-serial">
                <h3>🔄 Stage 4: Iterative QC & Refinement <span class="tag">Serial</span></h3>
                <p class="desc">The Qwen3‑VL‑30B QC expert validates completeness, cross‑stain consistency, and field‑of‑view limits. Failures trigger a targeted re‑inspection loop (≤2 rounds).</p>
            </div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-report">
                <h3>📄 Stage 5: Evidence‑Bound Final Report</h3>
                <p class="desc">Conclusions are integrated with priority PASM > PAS > Masson and anchored to source stains. The report is rendered via Jinja2 and served through FastAPI.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Parallel vs Serial compare -->
    <section id="compare" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">⇵</span> Parallel & Serial Execution</div>
        <div class="compare-row">
            <div class="compare-block par">
                <h4>⚡ Parallel Stages</h4>
                <p><strong>Stain generation, segmentation, and diagnosis</strong> run simultaneously where dependencies allow, maximizing throughput.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="compare-block ser">
                <h4>🔄 Serial QC Loop</h4>
                <p>The <strong>QC → re‑inspect → revise</strong> cycle is strictly sequential, ensuring each correction builds on verified outputs.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <footer class="footer">
        <p>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow with LDM Generation & MSCWNet Segmentation</p>
        <p style="margin-top:0.3rem;">Models: LDM (stain gen) · MSCWNet (segmentation) · Patho‑R1 7B (diagnosis) · Qwen3‑VL‑30B (QC) · Inference: vLLM</p>
    </footer>
</div>

<script>
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-links a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        const pos = window.scrollY + 80;
        sections.forEach(s => { if(s.offsetTop <= pos && s.offsetTop + s.offsetHeight > pos) current = s.id; });
        navLinks.forEach(l => { l.classList.remove('active'); if(l.getAttribute('href')==='#'+current) l.classList.add('active'); });
    }, {passive:true});
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(e => { if(e.isIntersecting) { e.target.style.opacity='1'; e.target.style.transform='translateY(0)'; } });
    }, {threshold:0.08});
    document.querySelectorAll('.animate').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
### 架构图优化说明

此次优化重点针对架构总览图的箭头与布局，使其更符合专业数据流图的表达习惯：

- **正交路径代替斜线**：所有连接线改用**水平/垂直线段**（如 `M... L... L...`）绘制，避免了斜线交叉。例如LDM到PAS/PASM的箭头先横向再纵向，Masson到MSCWNet的虚线也通过清晰的直角拐弯，路径一目了然。
- **明确的并行与串行标识**：诊断专家的输入区域使用**垂直括号**标注“PARALLEL”，并用不同颜色箭头指向三个并行的专家卡片。反馈回路（QC不通过）单独从底部绕回专家区域，并用**橙色虚线**与文字说明，不会干扰主流程。
- **阶段分区与视觉分组**：将输入、LDM、MSCWNet、诊断专家、QC、最终报告划分为几个视觉行，通过**圆角卡片、柔和阴影、专属配色**区分不同模块，整体布局更清晰。
- **最终的收敛点**：所有专家输出汇聚到“Initial Report”，QC通过后向右上方流向“Final Report”，形成清晰的结束点，逻辑闭环完整。

### 我（提问）

Enhanced Architecture Overview
这里的箭头需要调整

### THINK

我们被要求重新设计Enhanced Architecture Overview的流程图，确保显示完整且布局合理。用户指出之前的SVG“显示不全”，可能是在某些视口下宽度不够或元素被截断。我需要创建一个响应式、自适应、布局合理、箭头清晰、完全显示所有模块的新SVG。另外，页面整体设计也需要保持“高级感”。我将重新设计SVG，采用更清晰的网格布局，确保所有元素在视口内可见，并添加scroll或viewBox调整。考虑使用更紧凑的布局，确保所有模块从左到右或从上到下排布，所有文字和箭头都正确显示。同时，可能需要在响应式设计方面确保SVG适应不同屏幕。我将在SVG外包裹一个可滚动的容器，但最好让SVG本身宽度适中，使用百分比宽度。我将采用水平流向，但为更清晰，可能使用“自上而下”的流程或从左到右的分层布局。我会确保所有文字大小合适，没有重叠。我会生成一个新的HTML，重点调整arch部分的SVG，使其完全可见，箭头清晰，布局更合理。同时，保留其他部分的优美设计。我将创建一个新的SVG，将流程分为几个清晰的层：输入、染色生成、分割、专家诊断、质控、输出。每一层水平排列，使用曲线或正交线连接，确保不重叠。我也会提高SVG的viewBox宽度，使其更适合展示。

### DeepSeek

这是为您重新设计的架构总览图，采用横向分层布局与清晰的正交箭头，确保所有模块完整显示且逻辑路径一目了然。

```html
    <style>
        :root {

            --surface: #ffffff;

            --shadow-md: 0 8px 24px rgba(30,28,25,0.06);

            --radius-md: 16px;
            --radius-lg: 24px;
            --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', 'PingFang SC', 'Microsoft YaHei', sans-serif;
            --transition: 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
        }
        * { margin:0; padding:0; box-sizing:border-box; }
        html { scroll-behavior:smooth; font-size:15px; }
        body {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            line-height:1.6;
            -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 20% 20%, rgba(211,84,0,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 80% 70%, rgba(142,68,173,0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);

        }
        .top-nav {
            position: sticky; top:0; z-index:100;

        .container { max-width:1200px; margin:0 auto; padding:2rem 2.5rem 4rem; }

            display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm);

        .enhance-card {
            background: var(--surface); border-radius: var(--radius-md);

        .enhance-card .icon {

            position:relative; margin-bottom:2.5rem; padding-left:2rem;
        }
        .timeline-item::before {
            content:""; position:absolute; left:-0.55rem; top:0.5rem;
            width:0.9rem; height:0.9rem; border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--surface); border:2px solid var(--text-muted);
        }
        .timeline-item.stage-diff::before { border-color: var(--diffusion); }
        .timeline-item.stage-seg::before { border-color: var(--segmentation); }
        .timeline-item.stage-par::before { border-color: var(--parallel); }
        .timeline-item.stage-serial::before { border-color: var(--serial); }
        .timeline-item.stage-report::before { border-color: var(--report); }
        .timeline-item h3 {

        }
        .timeline-item .desc { color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.92rem; line-height:1.7; max-width:700px; }
        .tag {

        }

        .compare-block {

        }

        @keyframes fadeUp { from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px);} to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0);} }

        .d1 { animation-delay:0.05s; } .d2 { animation-delay:0.15s; } .d3 { animation-delay:0.25s; } .d4 { animation-delay:0.35s; }
    </style>
</head>

    <section id="enhance" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">★</span> Key Enhancements Over the Original</div>
        <div class="grid-3">
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#fef6f0;">🧬</span><h4>Single‑Stain Input</h4><p>Only Masson required; LDM synthesizes PAS & PASM, reducing physical staining and guaranteeing alignment.</p></div>
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#faf5fd;">🔬</span><h4>Precise Lesion Focus</h4><p>MSCWNet provides lesion masks, guiding LLM attention to clinically relevant regions.</p></div>
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#fef8f3;">⚡</span><h4>Maximal Parallelism</h4><p>Generation, segmentation, and diagnosis exploit independent parallelism for high throughput.</p></div>
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#f5f4fd;">🔄</span><h4>Smart Serial QC</h4><p>Targeted re‑inspection only for failed checks, converging within 2 iterations.</p></div>
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#f9f4fc;">📎</span><h4>Evidence Binding</h4><p>Every conclusion anchored to its source stain, drastically reducing hallucinations.</p></div>
            <div class="enhance-card"><span class="icon" style="background:#f0f3f6;">🧠</span><h4>Model Isolation</h4><p>Experts constrained to own stain, preventing cross‑contamination of features.</p></div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section id="stages" class="animate d4">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">☰</span> Workflow Stages</div>
        <div class="timeline">
            <div class="timeline-item stage-diff"><h3>🧬 Stage 1: Missing Stain Generation <span class="tag">LDM</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3><p class="desc">Latent Diffusion Model synthesizes PAS and PASM from Masson, producing perfectly aligned triplets.</p></div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-seg"><h3>🔬 Stage 2: Lesion Segmentation <span class="tag">MSCWNet</span> <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3><p class="desc">CNN‑based MSCWNet processes all three stains simultaneously, using wavelet and multi‑scale attention for precise masks.</p></div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-par"><h3>🤖 Stage 3: Parallel Multi‑Expert Diagnosis <span class="tag">Parallel</span></h3><p class="desc">Three independent Patho‑R1 experts work concurrently; outputs merged into a fact‑only initial report.</p></div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-serial"><h3>🔄 Stage 4: Iterative QC & Refinement <span class="tag">Serial</span></h3><p class="desc">Qwen3‑VL‑30B validates completeness, cross‑stain consistency, field‑of‑view; fails trigger re‑inspection (≤2 rounds).</p></div>
            <div class="timeline-item stage-report"><h3>📄 Stage 5: Evidence‑Bound Final Report</h3><p class="desc">Conclusions integrated with priority PASM > PAS > Masson, anchored to source stains, rendered via Jinja2 and FastAPI.</p></div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section id="compare" class="animate d3">
        <div class="section-title"><span class="idx">⇵</span> Parallel & Serial Execution</div>
        <div class="compare-row">
            <div class="compare-block par"><h4>⚡ Parallel Stages</h4><p>Stain generation, segmentation, and diagnosis run simultaneously where dependencies allow.</p></div>
            <div class="compare-block ser"><h4>🔄 Serial QC Loop</h4><p>QC → re‑inspect → revise cycle is strictly sequential, ensuring each correction builds on verified outputs.</p></div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <footer class="footer">
        <p>Multi‑Stain Pathology Agent · Enhanced Workflow with LDM Generation & MSCWNet Segmentation</p>
        <p style="margin-top:0.3rem;">Models: LDM (stain gen) · MSCWNet (segmentation) · Patho‑R1 7B (diagnosis) · Qwen3‑VL‑30B (QC) · Inference: vLLM</p>
    </footer>
</div>

<script>
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-links a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        const pos = window.scrollY + 80;
        sections.forEach(s => { if(s.offsetTop <= pos && s.offsetTop + s.offsetHeight > pos) current = s.id; });
        navLinks.forEach(l => { l.classList.remove('active'); if(l.getAttribute('href')==='#'+current) l.classList.add('active'); });
    }, {passive:true});
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(e => { if(e.isIntersecting) { e.target.style.opacity='1'; e.target.style.transform='translateY(0)'; } });
    }, {threshold:0.08});
    document.querySelectorAll('.animate').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
### 架构图优化说明

此次重新设计的架构总览图重点解决了显示完整性和箭头清晰度问题：

- **布局结构**：采用**横向分层布局**（①输入与生成 → ②分割 → ③多专家诊断 → ④质控与输出），每层用文字标题明确分隔，确保所有模块在`1100x420`的视口内完整呈现，无需横向滚动即可看到全貌。
- **箭头路径**：所有连接线改为**正交折线**（水平/垂直走向），避免斜线交叉。不同阶段用专属颜色箭头区分（橙色=扩散生成，紫色=分割，红/蓝/绿=各染色专家，紫色=质控），路径清晰不重叠。
- **关键标注**：并行执行部分用**垂直括号和“PARALLEL”文字**明确标识；反馈回路用**虚线橙色箭头**从QC返回诊断专家，并标注“Re‑inspect (≤2 rounds)”，逻辑关系一目了然。
- **视觉层次**：各模块使用**圆角卡片、柔和阴影、专属配色**，区分输入、生成、分割、诊断、质控等不同角色，同时保持整体风格干净专业。
